HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_31bd9757ddfa2818ea3d","text":"Later 32:29 answered the question by disclosing the opening's temporal function rather than choosing only judgment or victory: at its arrival, faith no longer benefits and no respite is granted. 32:30's command to wait then denied the challengers control of the timetable they had demanded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B001","root_000852/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_366302ed44c313835ceb","text":"2. **The challenge turns truth into a demand for a timetable.** `ص د ق:B001` requires correspondence between speech, inward stance, and reported reality; `ص د ق:B004` adds fulfillment that confirms a claim in action. *Matā* asks for time, and *in kuntum ṣādiqīn* makes the speakers' truth-status conditional on that requested arrival. **Inferred arrow:** warning claim -> demand for dated realization -> attempted control of the criterion of truth. They do not answer the signs in 32:26-27; they substitute a deadline test.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B001","root_001124/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3be1f8db9baa522dbc05","text":"1. **“Opening” deliberately holds several terminal functions together.** The basal `ف ت ح:B001` is opening what was closed; `ف ت ح:B003` is adjudication that settles a closed dispute, and `ف ت ح:B004` is victory as an opened outcome. 32:25 has just named final separation of disagreement. **Reading A:** they ask when the dispute will be judicially opened. **Reading B:** they ask when victory will open. The deictic *hādhā* leaves both active, and the next ayah answers by the event's effect rather than narrowing it to one gloss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3dd3d684ff5fd81e5cc9","text":"The answer in 32:29 preserved judgment and victory together but removed the fruitful side of opening: unlike released water, this opening does not generate a new usable interval for those who rejected. The reciprocal waiting in 32:30 showed that the consequence remains temporally withheld before that release.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B001","root_001124/B003","root_001272/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_44aa3d74fedf521ce9cc","text":"1. **Demand for the dispute to be opened by judgment.** **ف ت ح B003** supplies settling a closed dispute by judgment; **ق و ل B009** supplies mutual verbal negotiation; **ص د ق B001** supplies correspondence between speech and reality. Their question challenges the speakers to produce the event that will close the argument. I infer that الفتح is the adjudicative opening of a dispute presently kept in words. The demand reads as a request for truth to become public decision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_57a35b2cf98faca5b3d4","text":"3. **They ask for the terminal opening while standing after two present disclosures.** `ف ت ح:B008` permits an inner opening through insight or disclosure. The preceding ayat have made ruined dwellings into signs and barren land into visible renewal, yet the speakers ask only *matā hādhā al-fatḥ*. **Inferred arrow:** available disclosures fail to open insight -> attention is displaced onto one future decisive event -> present evidence is suspended. The question can therefore function as delay even while sounding like a request for verification.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_60f16b28b8d910300ce8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:28:4\",\"root_001124\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bir önceki ayetteki su akışının ardından hüküm sözcüğünün kanala salınan su anlamı, beklenen sonucu bir engel açılınca tarihe boşalacak bir akış gibi düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_64814c9fb4e20857d71c","text":"3. **A blocked consequence imagined as released flow (exploratory).** **ف ت ح B005** supplies water released from an opening into channels and irrigation.
